## Idea

We introduce the category of dependent type theories, following Voevodsky's
notion of $B$-systems. The category of generalized algebraic theories is defined
to be this category. It should be equivalent to the category of essentially
algebraic theories.

## (Dependency) systems

(Dependency) systems are the structure around which a dependent type theory is
built.

```text
    Ã₀       Ã₁       Ã₂
    |        |        |
    |        |        |
    ∨        ∨        ∨
    A₀ <---- A₁ <---- A₂ <---- ⋯
```

### Heterogeneous homotopies of sections of fibered systems

will introduce homotopies of sections of fibered systems. However, in order to
define concatenation of those homotopies, we will first define heterogeneous
homotopies of sections of fibered systems.

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="dependent.tr-fibered-system-slice"></a><a id="2501" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2501" class="Function">tr-fibered-system-slice</a> <a id="2525" class="Symbol">:</a>
    <a id="2531" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="2532" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2532" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="2535" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2535" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="2538" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2538" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="2541" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2541" class="Bound">l4</a> <a id="2544" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2546" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="2551" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="2553" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="2554" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2554" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="2556" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2558" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="2565" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2532" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="2568" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2535" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="2570" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="2572" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="2573" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2573" class="Bound">B</a> <a id="2575" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2575" class="Bound">B&#39;</a> <a id="2578" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="2580" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1431" class="Record">fibered-system</a> <a id="2595" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2538" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="2598" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2541" class="Bound">l4</a> <a id="2601" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2554" class="Bound">A</a><a id="2602" class="Symbol">}</a>

### Nonhomogenous homotopies

We specialize the above definitions to nonhomogenous homotopies.

    <a id="8519" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="8520" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8520" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="8523" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8523" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="8526" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8526" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="8529" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8529" class="Bound">l4</a> <a id="8532" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="8534" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="8539" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="8541" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="8542" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8542" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="8544" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="8546" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="8553" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8520" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="8556" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8523" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="8558" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="8560" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="8561" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8561" class="Bound">B</a> <a id="8563" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="8565" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="8572" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8526" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="8575" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8529" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="8577" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="8579" class="Symbol">→</a>
    <a id="8585" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="8588" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="8589" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8520" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="8592" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="8594" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8523" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="8597" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="8599" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8526" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="8602" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="8604" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8529" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="8606" class="Symbol">)</a>

    <a id="8631" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1796" class="Record">section-system</a> <a id="8646" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="8647" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8099" class="Function">constant-fibered-system</a> <a id="8671" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8621" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="8673" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8623" class="Bound">B</a><a id="8674" class="Symbol">)</a>
</pre>
### Homotopies of morphisms of systems

Homotopies of morphisms of systems are defined as an instance of homotopies of
sections of fibered systems.

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="dependent.htpy-hom-system"></a><a id="8840" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8840" class="Function">htpy-hom-system</a> <a id="8856" class="Symbol">:</a>
    <a id="8862" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="8863" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8863" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="8866" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8866" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="8869" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8869" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="8872" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8872" class="Bound">l4</a> <a id="8875" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="8877" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="8882" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="8884" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="8885" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8885" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="8887" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="8889" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="8896" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8863" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="8899" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8866" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="8901" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="8903" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="8904" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8904" class="Bound">B</a> <a id="8906" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="8908" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="8915" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8869" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="8918" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8872" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="8920" class="Symbol">}</a>
    <a id="8926" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="8927" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8927" class="Bound">f</a> <a id="8929" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8929" class="Bound">g</a> <a id="8931" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="8933" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8502" class="Function">hom-system</a> <a id="8944" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8885" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="8946" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8904" class="Bound">B</a><a id="8947" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="8949" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="8951" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="8954" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="8955" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8863" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="8958" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="8960" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8866" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="8963" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="8965" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8869" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="8968" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="8970" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8872" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="8972" class="Symbol">)</a>
  <a id="8976" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8840" class="Function">htpy-hom-system</a> <a id="8992" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8992" class="Bound">f</a> <a id="8994" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8994" class="Bound">g</a> <a id="8996" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="8998" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#6393" class="Function">htpy-section-system</a> <a id="9018" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8992" class="Bound">f</a> <a id="9020" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8994" class="Bound">g</a>

## The category of systems

We show that systems form a category.

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="dependent.id-hom-system"></a><a id="9768" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#9768" class="Function">id-hom-system</a> <a id="9782" class="Symbol">:</a>
    <a id="9788" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="9789" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#9789" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="9792" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#9792" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="9795" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="9797" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="9802" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="9804" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="9805" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#9805" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="9807" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="9809" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="9816" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#9789" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="9819" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#9792" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="9821" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="9823" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="9825" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8502" class="Function">hom-system</a> <a id="9836" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#9805" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="9838" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#9805" class="Bound">A</a>

---

## Structures on dependent type theories

Dependent type theories are systems equipped with weakening and substitution
structure, and with the structure of generic elements (the variable rule).

### Weakening structure on systems

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="17253" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="dependent.weakening"></a><a id="17260" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17260" class="Record">weakening</a> <a id="17270" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="17271" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17271" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="17274" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17274" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="17277" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17279" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="17284" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="17286" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17287" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17287" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="17289" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17291" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="17298" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17271" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="17301" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17274" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="17303" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="17305" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17307" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="17310" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17311" href="Agda.Primitive.html#931" class="Primitive">lsuc</a> <a id="17316" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17271" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="17319" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="17321" href="Agda.Primitive.html#931" class="Primitive">lsuc</a> <a id="17326" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17274" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="17328" class="Symbol">)</a>
    <a id="17334" class="Keyword">where</a>
    <a id="17344" class="Keyword">coinductive</a>
    <a id="17360" class="Keyword">field</a>
      <a id="dependent.weakening.type"></a><a id="17372" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17372" class="Field">type</a> <a id="17377" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17379" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17380" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17380" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="17382" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17384" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="17396" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17287" class="Bound">A</a><a id="17397" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="17399" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="17401" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8502" class="Function">hom-system</a> <a id="17412" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17287" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="17414" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17415" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1387" class="Field">system.slice</a> <a id="17428" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17287" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="17430" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17380" class="Bound">X</a><a id="17431" class="Symbol">)</a>
      <a id="dependent.weakening.slice"></a><a id="17439" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17439" class="Field">slice</a> <a id="17445" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17447" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17448" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17448" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="17450" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17452" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="17464" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17287" class="Bound">A</a><a id="17465" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="17467" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="17469" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17260" class="Record">weakening</a> <a id="17479" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17480" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1387" class="Field">system.slice</a> <a id="17493" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17287" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="17495" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17448" class="Bound">X</a><a id="17496" class="Symbol">)</a>
</pre>
### Morphisms preserving weakening structure

We state what it means for a morphism to preserve weakening structure.

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="17631" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="dependent.preserves-weakening"></a><a id="17638" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17638" class="Record">preserves-weakening</a>
    <a id="17662" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="17663" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17663" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="17666" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17666" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="17669" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17669" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="17672" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17672" class="Bound">l4</a> <a id="17675" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17677" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="17682" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="17684" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="17685" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17685" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="17687" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17689" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="17696" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17663" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="17699" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17666" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="17701" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="17703" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="17704" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17704" class="Bound">B</a> <a id="17706" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17708" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="17715" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17669" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="17718" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17672" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="17720" class="Symbol">}</a>
    <a id="17726" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17727" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17727" class="Bound">WA</a> <a id="17730" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17732" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17260" class="Record">weakening</a> <a id="17742" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17685" class="Bound">A</a><a id="17743" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="17745" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17746" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17746" class="Bound">WB</a> <a id="17749" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17751" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17260" class="Record">weakening</a> <a id="17761" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17704" class="Bound">B</a><a id="17762" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="17764" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17765" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17765" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="17767" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17769" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8502" class="Function">hom-system</a> <a id="17780" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17685" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="17782" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17704" class="Bound">B</a><a id="17783" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="17785" class="Symbol">:</a>
    <a id="17791" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="17794" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17795" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17663" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="17798" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="17800" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17666" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="17803" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="17805" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17669" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="17808" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="17810" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17672" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="17812" class="Symbol">)</a>
    <a id="17818" class="Keyword">where</a>
    <a id="17828" class="Keyword">coinductive</a>
    <a id="17844" class="Keyword">field</a>
      <a id="dependent.preserves-weakening.type"></a><a id="17856" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17856" class="Field">type</a> <a id="17861" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="17871" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="17872" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17872" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="17874" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="17876" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="17888" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17685" class="Bound">A</a><a id="17889" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="17891" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="17901" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8840" class="Function">htpy-hom-system</a>
          <a id="17927" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="17929" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#10016" class="Function">comp-hom-system</a>
            <a id="17957" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="17959" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2130" class="Field">section-system.slice</a> <a id="17980" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17765" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="17982" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17872" class="Bound">X</a><a id="17983" class="Symbol">)</a>
            <a id="17997" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="17999" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17372" class="Field">weakening.type</a> <a id="18014" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17727" class="Bound">WA</a> <a id="18017" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17872" class="Bound">X</a><a id="18018" class="Symbol">))</a>
          <a id="18031" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="18033" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#10016" class="Function">comp-hom-system</a>
            <a id="18061" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="18063" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17372" class="Field">weakening.type</a> <a id="18078" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17746" class="Bound">WB</a> <a id="18081" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18082" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1956" class="Field">section-system.type</a> <a id="18102" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17765" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="18104" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17872" class="Bound">X</a><a id="18105" class="Symbol">))</a>
            <a id="18120" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="18122" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17765" class="Bound">h</a><a id="18123" class="Symbol">))</a>
      <a id="dependent.preserves-weakening.slice"></a><a id="18132" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18132" class="Field">slice</a> <a id="18138" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="18148" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18149" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18149" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="18151" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18153" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="18165" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17685" class="Bound">A</a><a id="18166" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="18168" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="18178" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17638" class="Record">preserves-weakening</a>
          <a id="18208" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="18210" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17439" class="Field">weakening.slice</a> <a id="18226" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17727" class="Bound">WA</a> <a id="18229" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18149" class="Bound">X</a><a id="18230" class="Symbol">)</a>
          <a id="18242" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="18244" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17439" class="Field">weakening.slice</a> <a id="18260" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17746" class="Bound">WB</a> <a id="18263" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18264" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1956" class="Field">section-system.type</a> <a id="18284" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17765" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="18286" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18149" class="Bound">X</a><a id="18287" class="Symbol">))</a>
          <a id="18300" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="18302" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2130" class="Field">section-system.slice</a> <a id="18323" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17765" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="18325" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18149" class="Bound">X</a><a id="18326" class="Symbol">)</a>
</pre>
### Substitution structure on systems

We introduce substitution structure on a system.

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="18432" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="dependent.substitution"></a><a id="18439" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18439" class="Record">substitution</a> <a id="18452" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="18453" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18453" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="18456" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18456" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="18459" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18461" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="18466" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="18468" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18469" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18469" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="18471" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18473" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="18480" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18453" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="18483" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18456" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="18485" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="18487" class="Symbol">:</a>
    <a id="18493" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="18496" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18497" href="Agda.Primitive.html#931" class="Primitive">lsuc</a> <a id="18502" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18453" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="18505" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="18507" href="Agda.Primitive.html#931" class="Primitive">lsuc</a> <a id="18512" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18456" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="18514" class="Symbol">)</a>
    <a id="18520" class="Keyword">where</a>
    <a id="18530" class="Keyword">coinductive</a>
    <a id="18546" class="Keyword">field</a>
      <a id="dependent.substitution.type"></a><a id="18558" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18558" class="Field">type</a> <a id="18563" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="18573" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="18574" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18574" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="18576" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18578" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="18590" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18469" class="Bound">A</a><a id="18591" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="18593" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18594" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18594" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="18596" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18598" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1358" class="Field">system.element</a> <a id="18613" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18469" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="18615" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18574" class="Bound">X</a><a id="18616" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="18618" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="18628" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8502" class="Function">hom-system</a> <a id="18639" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18640" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1387" class="Field">system.slice</a> <a id="18653" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18469" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="18655" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18574" class="Bound">X</a><a id="18656" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="18658" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18469" class="Bound">A</a>
      <a id="dependent.substitution.slice"></a><a id="18666" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18666" class="Field">slice</a> <a id="18672" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18674" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18675" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18675" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="18677" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18679" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="18691" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18469" class="Bound">A</a><a id="18692" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="18694" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="18696" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18439" class="Record">substitution</a> <a id="18709" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18710" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1387" class="Field">system.slice</a> <a id="18723" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18469" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="18725" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18675" class="Bound">X</a><a id="18726" class="Symbol">)</a>
</pre>
### Morphisms preserving substitution structure

We state what it means for a morphism to preserve substitution structure.

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="18867" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="dependent.preserves-substitution"></a><a id="18874" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18874" class="Record">preserves-substitution</a>
    <a id="18901" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="18902" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18902" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="18905" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18905" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="18908" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18908" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="18911" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18911" class="Bound">l4</a> <a id="18914" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18916" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="18921" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="18923" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="18924" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18924" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="18926" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18928" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="18935" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18902" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="18938" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18905" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="18940" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="18942" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="18943" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18943" class="Bound">B</a> <a id="18945" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18947" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="18954" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18908" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="18957" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18911" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="18959" class="Symbol">}</a>
    <a id="18965" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18966" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18966" class="Bound">SA</a> <a id="18969" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18971" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18439" class="Record">substitution</a> <a id="18984" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18924" class="Bound">A</a><a id="18985" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="18987" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="18988" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18988" class="Bound">SB</a> <a id="18991" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="18993" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18439" class="Record">substitution</a> <a id="19006" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18943" class="Bound">B</a><a id="19007" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="19009" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="19010" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19010" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="19012" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="19014" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8502" class="Function">hom-system</a> <a id="19025" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18924" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="19027" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18943" class="Bound">B</a><a id="19028" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="19030" class="Symbol">:</a>
    <a id="19036" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="19039" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="19040" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18902" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="19043" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="19045" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18905" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="19048" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="19050" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18908" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="19053" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="19055" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18911" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="19057" class="Symbol">)</a>
    <a id="19063" class="Keyword">where</a>
    <a id="19073" class="Keyword">coinductive</a>
    <a id="19089" class="Keyword">field</a>
      <a id="dependent.preserves-substitution.type"></a><a id="19101" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19101" class="Field">type</a> <a id="19106" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="19116" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="19117" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19117" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="19119" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="19121" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="19133" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18924" class="Bound">A</a><a id="19134" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="19136" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="19137" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19137" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="19139" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="19141" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1358" class="Field">system.element</a> <a id="19156" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18924" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="19158" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19117" class="Bound">X</a><a id="19159" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="19161" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="19171" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8840" class="Function">htpy-hom-system</a>
          <a id="19197" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19199" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#10016" class="Function">comp-hom-system</a>
            <a id="19227" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19229" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19010" class="Bound">h</a><a id="19230" class="Symbol">)</a>
            <a id="19244" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19246" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18558" class="Field">substitution.type</a> <a id="19264" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18966" class="Bound">SA</a> <a id="19267" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19137" class="Bound">x</a><a id="19268" class="Symbol">))</a>
          <a id="19281" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19283" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#10016" class="Function">comp-hom-system</a>
            <a id="19311" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19313" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18558" class="Field">substitution.type</a> <a id="19331" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18988" class="Bound">SB</a>
              <a id="19348" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19350" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2015" class="Field">section-system.element</a> <a id="19373" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19010" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="19375" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19137" class="Bound">x</a><a id="19376" class="Symbol">))</a>
            <a id="19391" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19393" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2130" class="Field">section-system.slice</a> <a id="19414" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19010" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="19416" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19117" class="Bound">X</a><a id="19417" class="Symbol">))</a>
      <a id="dependent.preserves-substitution.slice"></a><a id="19426" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19426" class="Field">slice</a> <a id="19432" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="19442" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="19443" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19443" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="19445" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="19447" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="19459" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18924" class="Bound">A</a><a id="19460" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="19462" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="19472" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18874" class="Record">preserves-substitution</a>
          <a id="19505" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19507" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18666" class="Field">substitution.slice</a> <a id="19526" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18966" class="Bound">SA</a> <a id="19529" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19443" class="Bound">X</a><a id="19530" class="Symbol">)</a>
          <a id="19542" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19544" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18666" class="Field">substitution.slice</a> <a id="19563" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#18988" class="Bound">SB</a> <a id="19566" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="19567" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1956" class="Field">section-system.type</a> <a id="19587" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19010" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="19589" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19443" class="Bound">X</a><a id="19590" class="Symbol">))</a>
          <a id="19603" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="19605" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2130" class="Field">section-system.slice</a> <a id="19626" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19010" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="19628" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19443" class="Bound">X</a><a id="19629" class="Symbol">)</a>
</pre>
### The structure of a generic element on a system equipped with weakening structure

We introduce the structure of a generic element on a system equipped with
weakening structure.

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="19828" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="dependent.generic-element"></a><a id="19835" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19835" class="Record">generic-element</a>
    <a id="19855" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="19856" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19856" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="19859" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19859" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="19862" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="19864" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="19869" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="19871" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="19872" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19872" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="19874" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="19876" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="19883" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19856" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="19886" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19859" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="19888" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="19890" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="19891" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19891" class="Bound">W</a> <a id="19893" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="19895" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17260" class="Record">weakening</a> <a id="19905" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19872" class="Bound">A</a><a id="19906" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="19908" class="Symbol">:</a>
    <a id="19914" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="19917" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="19918" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19856" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="19921" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="19923" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19859" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="19925" class="Symbol">)</a>
    <a id="19931" class="Keyword">where</a>
    <a id="19941" class="Keyword">coinductive</a>
    <a id="19957" class="Keyword">field</a>
      <a id="dependent.generic-element.type"></a><a id="19969" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19969" class="Field">type</a> <a id="19974" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="19984" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="19985" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19985" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="19987" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="19989" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="20001" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#19872" class="Bound">A</a><a id="20002" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="20004" class="Symbol">→</a>

### Weakening and substitution morphisms preserve weakening, substitution, and generic elements

In a dependent type theory, every weakening morphism and every substitution
morphism preserve both the weakening and substitution structure, and they also
preserve generic elements.

For example, the rule that states that weakening preserves weakening (on types)
can be displayed as follows:

```text
        Γ ⊢ A type          Γ,Δ ⊢ B type          Γ,Δ,Ε ⊢ C type
  ------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Γ,A,W(A,Δ),W(A,B),W(W(A,B),W(A,E)) ⊢ W(W(A,B),W(A,C))=W(A,W(B,C)) type
```

Furthermore, there are laws that state that substitution by `a : A` cancels
weakening by `A`, that substituting a:A in the generic element of `A` gives us
the element a back, and that substituting by the generic element of `A` cancels
weakening by `A`.

We will now state these laws.

### Complete definition of a dependent type theory

We complete the definition of a dependent type theory.

### The slice of a dependent type theory

We introduce the slice of a dependent type theory.

        <a id="42454" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="42455" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#42455" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="42457" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="42459" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="42471" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="42472" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="42488" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#42366" class="Bound">A</a><a id="42489" class="Symbol">))</a> <a id="42492" class="Symbol">→</a>

### The condition that the action on elements of a morphism of dependent type theories is an equivalence

We introduce the condition that the action on elements of a morphism of
dependent type theories is an equivalence.

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="43103" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="dependent.is-equiv-on-elements-hom-system"></a><a id="43110" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43110" class="Record">is-equiv-on-elements-hom-system</a>
    <a id="43146" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="43147" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43147" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="43150" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43150" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="43153" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43153" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="43156" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43156" class="Bound">l4</a> <a id="43159" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="43161" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="43166" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="43168" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43169" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43169" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="43171" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="43173" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="43180" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43147" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="43183" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43150" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="43185" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="43187" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43188" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43188" class="Bound">B</a> <a id="43190" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="43192" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="43199" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43153" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="43202" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43156" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="43204" class="Symbol">)</a>
    <a id="43210" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43211" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43211" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="43213" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="43215" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#8502" class="Function">hom-system</a> <a id="43226" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43169" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="43228" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43188" class="Bound">B</a><a id="43229" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="43231" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="43233" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="43236" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43237" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43147" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="43240" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="43242" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43150" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="43245" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="43247" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43153" class="Bound">l3</a> <a id="43250" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="43252" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43156" class="Bound">l4</a><a id="43254" class="Symbol">)</a>
    <a id="43260" class="Keyword">where</a>
    <a id="43270" class="Keyword">coinductive</a>
    <a id="43286" class="Keyword">field</a>
      <a id="dependent.is-equiv-on-elements-hom-system.type"></a><a id="43298" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43298" class="Field">type</a> <a id="43303" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="43313" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43314" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43314" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="43316" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="43318" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="43330" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43169" class="Bound">A</a><a id="43331" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="43333" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="43335" href="foundation-core.equivalences.html#1532" class="Function">is-equiv</a> <a id="43344" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43345" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2015" class="Field">section-system.element</a> <a id="43368" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43211" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="43370" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="43371" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43314" class="Bound">X</a><a id="43372" class="Symbol">})</a>

        <a id="43397" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43398" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43398" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="43400" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="43402" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="43414" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43169" class="Bound">A</a><a id="43415" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="43417" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="43427" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43110" class="Record">is-equiv-on-elements-hom-system</a>

          <a id="43553" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="43555" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2130" class="Field">section-system.slice</a> <a id="43576" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43211" class="Bound">h</a> <a id="43578" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43398" class="Bound">X</a><a id="43579" class="Symbol">)</a>

### Unary type theories

        <a id="43832" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43833" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43833" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="43835" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43835" class="Bound">Y</a> <a id="43837" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="43839" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="43851" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43852" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="43868" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43667" class="Bound">A</a><a id="43869" class="Symbol">))</a> <a id="43872" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="43882" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43110" class="Record">is-equiv-on-elements-hom-system</a>
          <a id="43924" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="43926" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1387" class="Field">system.slice</a> <a id="43939" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="43940" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="43956" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43667" class="Bound">A</a><a id="43957" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="43959" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43835" class="Bound">Y</a><a id="43960" class="Symbol">)</a>
          <a id="43972" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="43974" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1387" class="Field">system.slice</a>
            <a id="43999" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="44001" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1387" class="Field">system.slice</a> <a id="44014" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44015" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="44031" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43667" class="Bound">A</a><a id="44032" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44034" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43833" class="Bound">X</a><a id="44035" class="Symbol">)</a>
            <a id="44049" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="44051" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1956" class="Field">section-system.type</a>
              <a id="44085" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="44087" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17372" class="Field">weakening.type</a> <a id="44102" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44103" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27672" class="Field">type-theory.W</a> <a id="44117" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43667" class="Bound">A</a><a id="44118" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44120" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43833" class="Bound">X</a><a id="44121" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44123" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43835" class="Bound">Y</a><a id="44124" class="Symbol">))</a>
          <a id="44137" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="44139" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#2130" class="Field">section-system.slice</a>
            <a id="44172" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="44174" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17372" class="Field">weakening.type</a> <a id="44189" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44190" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27672" class="Field">type-theory.W</a> <a id="44204" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43667" class="Bound">A</a><a id="44205" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44207" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43833" class="Bound">X</a><a id="44208" class="Symbol">)</a>
            <a id="44222" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="44224" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43835" class="Bound">Y</a><a id="44225" class="Symbol">))</a>

### Proof irrelevant type theories

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="44279" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="dependent.is-proof-irrelevant-type-theory"></a><a id="44286" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44286" class="Record">is-proof-irrelevant-type-theory</a>

    <a id="44391" class="Keyword">coinductive</a>
    <a id="44407" class="Keyword">field</a>

      <a id="dependent.is-proof-irrelevant-type-theory.slice"></a><a id="44535" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44535" class="Field">slice</a> <a id="44541" class="Symbol">:</a>

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="dependent.system-Slice"></a><a id="44667" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44667" class="Function">system-Slice</a> <a id="44680" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="44682" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="44683" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44683" class="Bound">l</a> <a id="44685" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="44687" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="44692" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="44694" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44695" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44695" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="44697" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="44699" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="44702" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44683" class="Bound">l</a><a id="44703" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44705" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="44707" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1253" class="Record">system</a> <a id="44714" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44715" href="Agda.Primitive.html#931" class="Primitive">lsuc</a> <a id="44720" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44683" class="Bound">l</a><a id="44721" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44723" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44683" class="Bound">l</a>
  <a id="44727" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="44739" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44740" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44667" class="Function">system-Slice</a> <a id="44753" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="44754" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44754" class="Bound">l</a><a id="44755" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="44757" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44757" class="Bound">X</a><a id="44758" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44760" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="44762" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44757" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="44764" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="44766" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="44769" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44754" class="Bound">l</a>
  <a id="44773" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1358" class="Field">system.element</a> <a id="44788" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44789" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44667" class="Function">system-Slice</a> <a id="44802" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44802" class="Bound">X</a><a id="44803" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44805" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44805" class="Bound">Y</a> <a id="44807" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="44809" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44810" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44810" class="Bound">x</a> <a id="44812" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="44814" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44802" class="Bound">X</a><a id="44815" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44817" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="44819" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44805" class="Bound">Y</a> <a id="44821" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44810" class="Bound">x</a>
  <a id="44825" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1387" class="Field">system.slice</a> <a id="44838" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44839" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44667" class="Function">system-Slice</a> <a id="44852" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44852" class="Bound">X</a><a id="44853" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="44855" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44855" class="Bound">Y</a> <a id="44857" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="44859" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44667" class="Function">system-Slice</a> <a id="44872" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="44873" href="foundation.dependent-pair-types.html#583" class="Record">Σ</a> <a id="44875" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44852" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="44877" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#44855" class="Bound">Y</a><a id="44878" class="Symbol">)</a>

  <a id="44883" class="Comment">{-
  hom-system-weakening-system-Slice :
    {l : Level} (X : UU l) (Y : X → UU l) →
    hom-system (system-Slice X) (system-Slice (Σ X Y))
  section-system.type (hom-system-weakening-system-Slice X Y) Z (pair x y) =
    Z x
  section-system.element
    (hom-system-weakening-system-Slice X Y) Z g (pair x y) =
    g x
  section-system.type
    (section-system.slice (hom-system-weakening-system-Slice X Y) Z)
    W (pair (pair x y) z) =
    W (pair x z)
  section-system.element
    (section-system.slice (hom-system-weakening-system-Slice X Y) Z)
    W h (pair (pair x y) z) =
    h (pair x z)
  section-system.slice
    (section-system.slice (hom-system-weakening-system-Slice X Y) Z) W =
    {!section-system.slice (hom-system-weakening-system-Slice X Y) ?!}

  weakening-system-Slice :
    {l : Level} (X : UU l) → weakening (system-Slice X)
  weakening.type (weakening-system-Slice X) Y =
    hom-system-weakening-system-Slice X Y
  weakening.slice (weakening-system-Slice X) = {!!}

  system-UU : (l : Level) → system (lsuc l) l
  system.type (system-UU l) = UU l
  system.element (system-UU l) X = X
  system.slice (system-UU l) X = system-Slice X

  weakening-type-UU :
    {l : Level} (X : UU l) →
    hom-system (system-UU l) (system.slice (system-UU l) X)
  section-system.type (weakening-type-UU X) Y x = Y
  section-system.element (weakening-type-UU X) Y y x = y
  section-system.slice (weakening-type-UU X) Y = {!!}

  weakening-UU : (l : Level) → weakening (system-UU l)
  section-system.type (weakening.type (weakening-UU l) X) Y x = Y
  section-system.element (weakening.type (weakening-UU l) X) Y y x = y
  section-system.type
    (section-system.slice (weakening.type (weakening-UU l) X) Y) Z t =
    Z (pr2 t)
  section-system.element
    ( section-system.slice (weakening.type (weakening-UU l) X) Y) Z f t =
    f (pr2 t)
  section-system.slice
    (section-system.slice (weakening.type (weakening-UU l) X) Y) Z =
    {!!}
  section-system.type
    ( weakening.type (weakening.slice (weakening-UU l) X) Y) Z (pair x y) =
    Z x
  section-system.element
    ( weakening.type (weakening.slice (weakening-UU l) X) Y) Z f (pair x y) =
    f x
  section-system.slice
    (weakening.type (weakening.slice (weakening-UU l) X) Y) Z =
    {!!}
  weakening.slice (weakening.slice (weakening-UU l) X) Y =
    weakening.slice (weakening-UU l) (Σ X Y)
-}</a>
</pre>

### Dependent type theories with Π-types

We define what it means for a dependent type theory to have Π-types.

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="47380" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="dependent.function-types"></a><a id="47387" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47387" class="Record">function-types</a>
    <a id="47406" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="47407" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47407" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="47410" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47410" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="47413" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="47415" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="47420" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="47422" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47423" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47423" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="47425" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="47427" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27564" class="Record">type-theory</a> <a id="47439" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47407" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="47442" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47410" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="47444" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="47446" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="47448" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="47451" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47452" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47407" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="47455" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="47457" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47410" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="47459" class="Symbol">)</a>
    <a id="47465" class="Keyword">where</a>
    <a id="47475" class="Keyword">coinductive</a>
    <a id="47491" class="Keyword">field</a>
      <a id="dependent.function-types.sys"></a><a id="47503" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47503" class="Field">sys</a> <a id="47507" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="47517" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47518" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47518" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="47520" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="47522" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="47534" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47535" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="47551" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47423" class="Bound">A</a><a id="47552" class="Symbol">))</a> <a id="47555" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="47565" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#30203" class="Record">hom-dtt</a> <a id="47573" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47574" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#28813" class="Function">slice-dtt</a> <a id="47584" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47423" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="47586" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47518" class="Bound">X</a><a id="47587" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="47589" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47423" class="Bound">A</a>
      <a id="dependent.function-types.app"></a><a id="47597" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47597" class="Field">app</a> <a id="47601" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="47611" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47612" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47612" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="47614" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="47616" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="47628" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47629" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="47645" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47423" class="Bound">A</a><a id="47646" class="Symbol">))</a> <a id="47649" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="47659" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#43110" class="Record">is-equiv-on-elements-hom-system</a>
          <a id="47701" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="47703" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="47719" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47720" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#28813" class="Function">slice-dtt</a> <a id="47730" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47423" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="47732" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47612" class="Bound">X</a><a id="47733" class="Symbol">))</a>
          <a id="47746" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="47748" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="47764" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47423" class="Bound">A</a><a id="47765" class="Symbol">)</a>
          <a id="47777" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="47779" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#30344" class="Field">hom-dtt.sys</a> <a id="47791" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47792" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47503" class="Field">sys</a> <a id="47796" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47612" class="Bound">X</a><a id="47797" class="Symbol">))</a>
      <a id="dependent.function-types.slice"></a><a id="47806" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47806" class="Field">slice</a> <a id="47812" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="47822" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47823" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47823" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="47825" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="47827" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="47839" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47840" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="47856" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47423" class="Bound">A</a><a id="47857" class="Symbol">))</a> <a id="47860" class="Symbol">→</a>
        <a id="47870" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47387" class="Record">function-types</a> <a id="47885" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="47886" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#28813" class="Function">slice-dtt</a> <a id="47896" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47423" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="47898" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47823" class="Bound">X</a><a id="47899" class="Symbol">)</a>

  <a id="47904" class="Comment">{-
  record preserves-function-types
    {l1 l2 l3 l4 : Level} {A : type-theory l1 l2}
    {B : type-theory l3 l4} (ΠA : function-types A)
    (ΠB : function-types B) (h : hom-dtt A B) : UU {!!}
    where
    coinductive
    field
      sys   : {!!}
      slice : {!!}
  -}</a>
</pre>

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="48198" class="Keyword">record</a> <a id="dependent.natural-numbers"></a><a id="48205" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48205" class="Record">natural-numbers</a>
    <a id="48225" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="48226" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48226" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="48229" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48229" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="48232" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="48234" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="48239" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="48241" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="48242" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48242" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="48244" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="48246" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27564" class="Record">type-theory</a> <a id="48258" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48226" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="48261" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48229" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="48263" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="48265" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="48266" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48266" class="Bound">Π</a> <a id="48268" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="48270" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47387" class="Record">function-types</a> <a id="48285" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48242" class="Bound">A</a><a id="48286" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="48288" class="Symbol">:</a>
    <a id="48294" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="48297" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="48298" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48226" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="48301" href="Agda.Primitive.html#961" class="Primitive Operator">⊔</a> <a id="48303" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48229" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="48305" class="Symbol">)</a>

    <a id="48321" class="Keyword">field</a>
      <a id="dependent.natural-numbers.N"></a><a id="48333" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48333" class="Field">N</a> <a id="48335" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="48337" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#28196" class="Function">closed-type-dtt</a> <a id="48353" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48242" class="Bound">A</a>
      <a id="dependent.natural-numbers.zero"></a><a id="48361" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48361" class="Field">zero</a> <a id="48366" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="48368" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#28323" class="Function">global-element-dtt</a> <a id="48387" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48242" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="48389" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48333" class="Field">N</a>
      <a id="dependent.natural-numbers.succ"></a><a id="48397" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48397" class="Field">succ</a> <a id="48402" class="Symbol">:</a>
        <a id="48412" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#28323" class="Function">global-element-dtt</a> <a id="48431" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48242" class="Bound">A</a>
          <a id="48443" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="48445" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1956" class="Field">section-system.type</a>
            <a id="48477" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="48479" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#30344" class="Field">hom-dtt.sys</a> <a id="48491" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="48492" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#47503" class="Field">function-types.sys</a> <a id="48511" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48266" class="Bound">Π</a> <a id="48513" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48333" class="Field">N</a><a id="48514" class="Symbol">))</a>
            <a id="48529" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="48531" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1956" class="Field">section-system.type</a>
              <a id="48565" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="48567" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#17372" class="Field">weakening.type</a> <a id="48582" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="48583" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27672" class="Field">type-theory.W</a> <a id="48597" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48242" class="Bound">A</a><a id="48598" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="48600" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48333" class="Field">N</a><a id="48601" class="Symbol">)</a>
              <a id="48617" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="48619" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#48333" class="Field">N</a><a id="48620" class="Symbol">)))</a>

  <a id="48627" class="Comment">{-
  natural-numbers-slice :
    {l1 l2 : Level} (A : type-theory l1 l2) (Π : function-types A)
    (N : natural-numbers A Π) (X : closed-type-dtt A) →
    natural-numbers (slice-dtt A X) (function-types.slice Π X)
  natural-numbers.N (natural-numbers-slice A Π N X) =
    section-system.type
      ( weakening.type (type-theory.W A) X)
      ( natural-numbers.N N)
  natural-numbers.zero (natural-numbers-slice A Π N X) =
    section-system.element
      ( weakening.type (type-theory.W A) X)
      ( natural-numbers.N N)
      ( natural-numbers.zero N)
  natural-numbers.succ (natural-numbers-slice A Π N X) =
    tr ( system.element (type-theory.sys (slice-dtt A X)))
       {! (section-system.type
          (preserves-weakening.type
          (hom-dtt.W (function-types.sys Π (natural-numbers.N N))) ?) ?)!}
    {-
    Id ( section-system.type
         ( weakening.type (type-theory.W A) X)
         ( section-system.type
           ( hom-dtt.sys (function-types.sys Π (natural-numbers.N N)))
           ( section-system.type
             ( weakening.type (type-theory.W A) (natural-numbers.N N))
             (natural-numbers.N N))))
       ( section-system.type
         ( hom-dtt.sys
           ( function-types.sys (function-types.slice Π X)
             ( natural-numbers.N (natural-numbers-slice A Π N X))))
         ( section-system.type
           ( weakening.type
             ( type-theory.W (slice-dtt A X))
             ( natural-numbers.N (natural-numbers-slice A Π N X)))
           ( natural-numbers.N (natural-numbers-slice A Π N X))))
  -}
       ( section-system.element
         ( weakening.type (type-theory.W A) X)
         ( section-system.type
           ( hom-dtt.sys (function-types.sys Π (natural-numbers.N N)))
           ( section-system.type
             ( weakening.type (type-theory.W A) (natural-numbers.N N))
             ( natural-numbers.N N)))
         ( natural-numbers.succ N))
  -}</a>
</pre>

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="50573" class="Comment">{-
  concat-htpy-hom-system&#39; :
    {l1 l2 l3 l4 : Level} {A : system l1 l2} {B B&#39; B&#39;&#39; : system l3 l4}
    (p : Id B B&#39;) (q : Id B&#39; B&#39;&#39;) {f : hom-system A B} {g : hom-system A B&#39;}
    {h : hom-system A B&#39;&#39;} → htpy-hom-system&#39; p f g → htpy-hom-system&#39; q g h →
    htpy-hom-system&#39; (p ∙ q) f h
  htpy-hom-system&#39;.type (concat-htpy-hom-system&#39; refl refl H K) =
    htpy-hom-system&#39;.type H ∙h htpy-hom-system&#39;.type K
  htpy-hom-system&#39;.element
    ( concat-htpy-hom-system&#39; {A = A} {B} {.B} refl refl {f} H K) X x =
    ( ( tr-concat
        ( section-system.type H X)
        ( section-system.type K X)
        ( section-system.element (tr (hom-system A) refl f) X x)) ∙
      ( ap
        ( tr (system.element B) (section-system.type K X))
        ( section-system.element H X x))) ∙
    ( section-system.element K X x)
  htpy-hom-system&#39;.slice (concat-htpy-hom-system&#39; p q H K) = {!!}

  concat-htpy-hom-system :
    {l1 l2 l3 l4 : Level} {A : system l1 l2} {B : system l3 l4}
    {f g h : hom-system A B} (H : htpy-hom-system f g)
    (K : htpy-hom-system g h) → htpy-hom-system f h
  htpy-hom-system&#39;.type (concat-htpy-hom-system H K) =
    section-system.type H ∙h section-system.type K
  htpy-hom-system&#39;.element
    ( concat-htpy-hom-system {A = A} {B = B} {f} H K) X x =
    ( ( tr-concat
        ( section-system.type H X)
        ( section-system.type K X)
        ( section-system.element (tr (hom-system A) refl f) X x)) ∙
      ( ap
        ( tr (system.element B) (section-system.type K X))
        ( section-system.element H X x))) ∙
    ( section-system.element K X x)
  htpy-hom-system&#39;.slice (concat-htpy-hom-system H K) X = {!!}
  -}</a>
</pre>

## Contexts in a dependent type theory

We interpret contexts in a dependent type theory.

<pre class="Agda"><a id="52331" class="Keyword">module</a> <a id="c-system"></a><a id="52338" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52338" class="Module">c-system</a> <a id="52347" class="Keyword">where</a>

  <a id="52356" class="Keyword">open</a> <a id="52361" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1227" class="Module">dependent</a>

  <a id="52374" class="Keyword">data</a> <a id="c-system.context"></a><a id="52379" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52379" class="Datatype">context</a>
    <a id="52391" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="52392" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52392" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="52395" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52395" class="Bound">l2</a> <a id="52398" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="52400" href="Agda.Primitive.html#742" class="Postulate">Level</a><a id="52405" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="52407" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="52408" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52408" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="52410" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="52412" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27564" class="Record">type-theory</a> <a id="52424" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52392" class="Bound">l1</a> <a id="52427" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52395" class="Bound">l2</a><a id="52429" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="52431" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="52433" href="Agda.Primitive.html#388" class="Primitive">UU</a> <a id="52436" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52392" class="Bound">l1</a>
    <a id="52443" class="Keyword">where</a>
    <a id="c-system.context.empty-ctx"></a><a id="52453" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52453" class="InductiveConstructor">empty-ctx</a> <a id="52463" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="52465" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52379" class="Datatype">context</a> <a id="52473" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52408" class="Bound">A</a>
    <a id="c-system.context.extension-ctx"></a><a id="52479" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52479" class="InductiveConstructor">extension-ctx</a> <a id="52493" class="Symbol">:</a>
      <a id="52501" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="52502" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52502" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="52504" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="52506" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="52518" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="52519" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="52535" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52408" class="Bound">A</a><a id="52536" class="Symbol">))</a>
      <a id="52545" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="52546" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52546" class="Bound">Γ</a> <a id="52548" class="Symbol">:</a> <a id="52550" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52379" class="Datatype">context</a> <a id="52558" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="52559" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#28813" class="Function">slice-dtt</a> <a id="52569" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52408" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="52571" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52502" class="Bound">X</a><a id="52572" class="Symbol">))</a> <a id="52575" class="Symbol">→</a> <a id="52577" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52379" class="Datatype">context</a> <a id="52585" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52408" class="Bound">A</a>

### Elements of contexts

<pre class="Agda"><a id="53032" class="Comment">{-
  data element-context
    {l1 l2 : Level} {A : type-theory l1 l2} :
    (Γ : context A) → UU {!substitution.type (type-theory.S A) !}
    where
    element-empty-context : element-context empty-ctx
    element-extension-ctx :
      {!(X : system.type (type-theory.sys A))
        (Γ : context (slice-dtt A X))
        (x : system.element (type-theory.sys A) X)
        (y : element-context
              (context-hom (substitution.type (type-theory.S A) x) Γ)) →
        element-context (extension-ctx X Γ)!}
-}</a>
</pre>
### Interpreting types in context in a dependent type theory

  <a id="53702" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53625" class="Function">type</a> <a id="53707" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53707" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="53709" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52453" class="InductiveConstructor">empty-ctx</a> <a id="53719" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="53721" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#1339" class="Field">system.type</a> <a id="53733" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="53734" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#27647" class="Field">type-theory.sys</a> <a id="53750" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53707" class="Bound">A</a><a id="53751" class="Symbol">)</a>
  <a id="53755" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53625" class="Function">type</a> <a id="53760" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53760" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="53762" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="53763" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#52479" class="InductiveConstructor">extension-ctx</a> <a id="53777" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53777" class="Bound">X</a> <a id="53779" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53779" class="Bound">Γ</a><a id="53780" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="53782" class="Symbol">=</a> <a id="53784" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53625" class="Function">type</a> <a id="53789" class="Symbol">(</a><a id="53790" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#28813" class="Function">slice-dtt</a> <a id="53800" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53760" class="Bound">A</a> <a id="53802" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53777" class="Bound">X</a><a id="53803" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="53805" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53779" class="Bound">Γ</a>
</pre>
### Interpreting elements of types in context in a dependent type theory

<pre class="Agda">  <a id="c-system.element"></a><a id="53896" href="type-theories.dependent-type-theories.html#53896" class="Function">element</a> <a id="53904" class="Symbol">:</a>

<a id="54429" class="Comment">{-
  weakening-by-type-context :
    {l1 l2 : Level} {A : type-theory l1 l2}
    (X : system.type (type-theory.sys A)) →
    context A → context (slice-dtt A X)

    context-hom {!weakening.type (type-theory.W A) X!} Δ
-}</a>

<a id="55083" class="Comment">{-
  weakening-context :
    {l1 l2 : Level} (A : type-theory l1 l2) (Γ : context A) →
    context A → context (slice A Γ)
  weakening-context A empty-ctx Δ = Δ
  weakening-context A (extension-ctx X Γ) empty-ctx = empty-ctx
  weakening-context A (extension-ctx X Γ) (extension-ctx Y Δ) =
    extension-ctx
      ( weakening-type-context A (extension-ctx X Γ) Y)
      ( weakening-context {!!} {!!} {!!})
-}</a>
